- [01:00:05](https://youtu.be/ZsDht8VcsyA?t=01h00m05s) Topic: Would you erase an embarrassing memory?
- [01:03:54](https://youtu.be/ZsDht8VcsyA?t=01h03m54s) Topic: What daily ritual would improve society?
